West of House
You are standing in an open field west of a white house, with a boarded front door.
There is a small mailbox here.


Starting location: West House
Target score: 80
Model: openai/gpt-4o-mini | Agent type: smart
Maximum steps: 500

LESSONS LEARNED FROM PREVIOUS EPISODES:
Repeat these positive behaviors:
1. Go North House→Behind House→'west' via the open window; the Kitchen entry scored +10. Skip the mailbox and forest detours.
2. Pick up the lantern and sword in the Living Room before descending; the sword is still unconfirmed after five runs.
3. Take the Gallery painting and put it in the trophy case: scored +4 then +6 this run, so bank it early.
4. Light the lantern right after any pitch-black arrival; the trapdoor descent worked and no dark death occurred this time.
5. After each action, compare score before and after and credit only the recorded delta; verify inventory and route before repeating any score hint.
Avoid these negative behaviors:
1. Do not 'jump' at East Chasm or try other risky verbs; it killed the Actor (-10) and ended the run at 35.
2. Do not cycle the same room pairs (Gallery↔Studio, East Chasm↔Cellar) after two zero-score passes; try a new action or exit.
3. Do not enter the Troll Room unarmed; it was visited twice, then retreated from, with no score or progress.
4. Do not waste turns on put/take manual, bottle, lunch or sack open/close shuffles; none gave score.
5. Do not trust stale 'up'/'down' hints or contradictory notes; re-read the room, exits and inventory before acting.
Apply these lessons when relevant; current game feedback takes precedence.

Historian episode review — episode 19
================================================

Review source: llm
Lessons: 5 positive + 5 negative (configured per category: 5)

Final score was 75 of 80 with no death, ending at the step limit. Confirmed net score events: kitchen +10, trapdoor +25, painting +4 then +6 in the case, Troll Room east exit +5, bar +10 then +5, egg +5 then +5. Sword and lantern were both carried, and the troll was passed. The lantern was never confirmed lit, though no dark death occurred. The Actor's strongest play was exploring beyond home, which found the bar and egg, and banking treasure quickly. After about step 404 the score stayed at 75, and the last ~100 moves were wasted. They looped Living Room↔Cellar and Kitchen↔Attic, repeatedly took treasures out of the case (-5/-6 each, restored on re-deposit, net zero), and churned the window, axe and leaves. A stale "'up' +15" hint kept driving this, and the Actor followed it without checking whether it still applied. Next episode: treat the plateau as a signal to visit unexplored exits, and never withdraw case items.

LESSONS LEARNED
================================
POSITIVES TO REPEAT
1. Open the window from Behind House and go west for +10, then take the trapdoor down for +25; both scored again and are reliable early.
2. Bank each treasure in the trophy case right after pickup: painting (+4/+6), bar (+10/+5) and egg (+5/+5) each scored once.
3. Carry the sword and lantern before going underground; armed Troll Room passage worked, and the east exit gave +5 with no death.
4. When score stalls, probe unvisited exits and rooms, such as maze branches and forest areas; the bar and egg both came from exploration.
5. Check inventory for a lit lantern before dark rooms, and credit only recorded score deltas; the lit state was never confirmed this run.

NEGATIVES TO AVOID
1. Never take a treasure out of the trophy case; each take cost -5 or -6 and re-depositing only restored it, about a dozen times for net zero.
2. After two zero-score passes, stop cycling Living Room↔Cellar or Kitchen↔Attic; roughly 100 moves after step 404 produced nothing.
3. Ignore stale "'up' +15" score hints when repeated 'up' probes at Cellar, Kitchen and Attic return 0; re-read the room and exits instead.
4. Don't churn open/close window or put/take axe, leaves and sack items; none of these ever changed the score.
5. Don't 'jump' at East Chasm or use other risky verbs; that killed an earlier run (-10).
